To make Hessian supervision practically viable, we develop a highly efficient training protocol, termed Hessian INformed Training (HINT), achieving two to four orders of magnitude reduction for the requirement of expensive Hessian labels. HINT integrates critical techniques, including Hessian pre-training, configuration sampling, curriculum learning and stochastic projection Hessian loss. Enabled by HINT, Hi-MLIP significantly improves transition-state search and brings Gibbs free-energy predictions close to chemical accuracy especially in data-scarce regimes. Our framework also enables accurate treatment of strongly anharmonic hydrides, reproducing phonon renormalization and superconducting critical temperatures in close agreement with experiment while bypassing the computational bottleneck of anharmonic calculations. These results establish a practical route to enhancing curvature awareness of machine learning interatomic potentials, bridging simulation and experimental observables across a wide range of systems.

</details>


### [150] [GlowQ: Group-Shared LOw-Rank Approximation for Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25385)
*Selim An,Il hong Suh,Yeseong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlowQ提出了一种分组共享低秩近似方法，通过缓存单个共享右因子来减少量化LLM的延迟和内存开销，同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化技术（如BitsAndBytes、AWQ、GPTQ）在低比特表示（如4位）下会降低精度，而低秩校正方法（如LQER、QERA、ASER）虽然能缓解此问题，但会恢复所有层并在每个解码器块中插入误差校正模块，导致延迟和内存开销增加。

Method: 提出GlowQ，一种分组共享低秩近似方法，为每个输入共享组缓存单个共享右因子，仅恢复提供最高精度收益的组或层。每个输入共享组计算一次高精度投影并在其模块中重复使用，减少参数和内存开销，同时保持层特定校正的表达能力。还提出选择性变体GlowQ-S，仅在提供最大收益的地方应用缓存的共享模块。

Result: 相比强基线，GlowQ平均减少TTFB 5.6%，增加吞吐量9.6%，在WikiText-2上减少困惑度0.17%，下游任务准确率提高0.42个百分点。选择性模型GlowQ-S进一步减少延迟，降低TTFB 23.4%，增加吞吐量37.4%，同时平均保持精度在0.2个百分点内。

Conclusion: GlowQ通过分组共享低秩近似有效解决了量化LLM的精度损失问题，在显著减少延迟和内存开销的同时保持或提高了模型精度，为高效部署大型语言模型提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Quantization techniques such as BitsAndBytes, AWQ, and GPTQ are widely used as a standard method in deploying large language models but often degrades accuracy when using low-bit representations, e.g., 4 bits. Low-rank correction methods (e.g., LQER, QERA, ASER) has been proposed to mitigate this issue, however, they restore all layers and insert error-correction modules into every decoder block, which increases latency and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GlowQ, a group-shared low-rank approximation for quantized LLMs that caches a single shared right factor per input-sharing group and restores only the groups or layers that yield the highest accuracy benefit. GlowQ computes the high-precision projection once per input-sharing group and reuses it across its modules, reducing parameter and memory overhead, and retaining the expressivity of layer-specific corrections. We also propose a selective variant, GlowQ-S, that applies the cached shared module only where it provides the largest benefit. Compared with strong baselines, our approach reduces TTFB by (5.6%) and increases throughput by (9.6%) on average, while reducing perplexity on WikiText-2 by (0.17%) and increasing downstream accuracy by 0.42 percentage points. The selective model GlowQ-S further reduces latency, cutting TTFB by (23.4%) and increasing throughput by (37.4%), while maintaining accuracy within 0.2 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [151] [Maximum Entropy Behavior Exploration for Sim2Real Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25464)
*Jiajun Hu,Nuria Armengol Urpi,Jin Cheng,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FB-MEBE：一种结合无监督行为探索和正则化批评器的在线零样本强化学习算法，用于四足机器人控制，通过最大化行为分布熵来提升探索多样性，并生成可直接部署到硬件的自然策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本RL需要预收集相关多样的数据集，但在不了解下游任务的情况下难以获得高质量数据。现有方法在四足机器人控制中，无导向探索产生低多样性数据，导致下游性能差且策略无法直接硬件部署。

Method: FB-MEBE算法结合无监督行为探索策略和正则化批评器：1) 通过最大化实现行为分布的熵来促进探索；2) 使用正则化批评器将恢复的策略塑造成更自然、物理上合理的行为。

Result: FB-MEBE在模拟下游任务中相比其他探索策略获得改进性能，且生成的自然策略无需进一步微调即可无缝部署到硬件。

Conclusion: FB-MEBE通过结合行为熵最大化和正则化批评器，解决了在线零样本RL中探索多样性和策略自然性的问题，实现了可直接硬件部署的有效四足机器人控制策略。

Abstract: Zero-shot reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms aim to learn a family of policies from a reward-free dataset, and recover optimal policies for any reward function directly at test time. Naturally, the quality of the pretraining dataset determines the performance of the recovered policies across tasks. However, pre-collecting a relevant, diverse dataset without prior knowledge of the downstream tasks of interest remains a challenge. In this work, we study $\textit{online}$ zero-shot RL for quadrupedal control on real robotic systems, building upon the Forward-Backward (FB) algorithm. We observe that undirected exploration yields low-diversity data, leading to poor downstream performance and rendering policies impractical for direct hardware deployment. Therefore, we introduce FB-MEBE, an online zero-shot RL algorithm that combines an unsupervised behavior exploration strategy with a regularization critic. FB-MEBE promotes exploration by maximizing the entropy of the achieved behavior distribution. Additionally, a regularization critic shapes the recovered policies toward more natural and physically plausible behaviors. We empirically demonstrate that FB-MEBE achieves and improved performance compared to other exploration strategies in a range of simulated downstream tasks, and that it renders natural policies that can be seamlessly deployed to hardware without further finetuning. Videos and code available on our website.

</details>


### [152] [Not a fragment, but the whole: Map-based evaluation of data-driven Fire Danger Index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25469)
*Shahbaz Alvi,Italo Epicoco,Jose Maria Costa Saura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种与真实决策对齐的森林火灾预报模型评估新方法，系统评估火灾活动预测准确性和误报率，并证明ML模型集成能改善火灾识别和减少误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法预测野火发生的研究虽多，但标准分类器评估指标对火灾危险指数(FDI)预测的操作性能衡量有限，且评估常未充分考虑误报率这一操作关键因素

Method: 重新审视每日FDI模型评估范式，提出与真实世界决策对齐的森林火灾预报模型评估新方法，系统评估火灾活动预测准确性和误报率，并采用ML模型集成方法

Result: ML模型集成能同时改善火灾识别能力和减少误报，提出的评估方法能更准确地反映模型在实际操作中的性能

Conclusion: 需要与真实决策对齐的评估范式来全面评估森林火灾预报模型，ML模型集成是提升火灾识别和减少误报的有效策略

Abstract: A growing body of literature has focused on predicting wildfire occurrence using machine learning methods, capitalizing on high-resolution data and fire predictors that canonical process-based frameworks largely ignore. Standard evaluation metrics for an ML classifier, while important, provide a potentially limited measure of the model's operational performance for the Fire Danger Index (FDI) forecast. Furthermore, model evaluation is frequently conducted without adequately accounting for false positive rates, despite their critical relevance in operational contexts. In this paper, we revisit the daily FDI model evaluation paradigm and propose a novel method for evaluating a forest fire forecasting model that is aligned with real-world decision-making. Furthermore, we systematically assess performance in accurately predicting fire activity and the false positives (false alarms). We further demonstrate that an ensemble of ML models improves both fire identification and reduces false positives.

</details>


### [153] [Causal-INSIGHT: Probing Temporal Models to Extract Causal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25473)
*Benjamin Redden,Hui Wang,Shuyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Causal-INSIGHT是一个模型无关的后处理解释框架，用于从训练好的时序预测器中提取模型隐含的、有向的、有时滞的影响结构，通过干预式输入钳制分析预测器的响应，并使用Qbic准则进行稀疏图选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多元时间序列中的有向时序交互对于解释复杂动态系统及其预测模型至关重要。现有方法通常关注数据生成过程的因果结构推断，而本文关注从已训练预测器中提取模型依赖的影响结构。

Method: 提出Causal-INSIGHT框架：1）在推理时对预训练预测器应用系统性的、受干预启发的输入钳制；2）从预测器的响应中构建有向时序影响信号；3）引入Qbic（稀疏感知图选择准则），在不需要真实图标签的情况下平衡预测保真度和结构复杂性。

Result: 在合成、模拟和现实基准测试中，Causal-INSIGHT能够泛化到不同的骨干架构，保持竞争力的结构准确性，并在应用于现有预测器时显著改善时序延迟定位性能。

Conclusion: Causal-INSIGHT提供了一个有效的后处理解释框架，能够从训练好的时序预测器中提取模型依赖的有向时序影响结构，通过Qbic准则实现稀疏图选择，为复杂动态系统的预测模型提供可解释性分析。

Abstract: Understanding directed temporal interactions in multivariate time series is essential for interpreting complex dynamical systems and the predictive models trained on them. We present Causal-INSIGHT, a model-agnostic, post-hoc interpretation framework for extracting model-implied (predictor-dependent), directed, time-lagged influence structure from trained temporal predictors. Rather than inferring causal structure at the level of the data-generating process, Causal-INSIGHT analyzes how a fixed, pre-trained predictor responds to systematic, intervention-inspired input clamping applied at inference time.
  From these responses, we construct directed temporal influence signals that reflect the dependencies the predictor relies on for prediction, and introduce Qbic, a sparsity-aware graph selection criterion that balances predictive fidelity and structural complexity without requiring ground-truth graph labels. Experiments across synthetic, simulated, and realistic benchmarks show that Causal-INSIGHT generalizes across diverse backbone architectures, maintains competitive structural accuracy, and yields significant improvements in temporal delay localization when applied to existing predictors.

</details>


### [154] [How Class Ontology and Data Scale Affect Audio Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25476)
*Manuel Milling,Andreas Triantafyllopoulos,Alexander Gebhard,Simon Rampp,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了音频到音频迁移学习的内部机制，通过在不同预训练数据集上预训练模型并在三个下游任务上微调，发现预训练数据与下游任务的相似性对迁移学习效果影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管迁移学习在深度学习中广泛应用且效果显著，但其内部工作机制仍存在许多未解之谜，特别是关于何时以及为何迁移学习有效的问题。本研究旨在通过音频到音频迁移学习的系统实验来深入理解迁移学习的机制。

Method: 在AudioSet的（基于本体的）子集上预训练各种模型状态，然后在三个计算机听觉任务上进行微调：声学场景识别、鸟类活动识别和语音命令识别。通过控制预训练数据的样本数量和类别数量，研究这些因素对迁移学习效果的影响。

Result: 增加预训练数据的样本数量和类别数量都对迁移学习有积极影响。然而，预训练数据与下游任务之间的相似性通常具有更大的影响，这种相似性可以使模型学习到可比较的特征。

Conclusion: 迁移学习的有效性不仅取决于预训练数据的规模和多样性，更重要的是预训练数据与目标任务的相似性。这一发现为理解迁移学习机制提供了重要见解，并有助于指导实际应用中预训练策略的选择。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a crucial concept within deep learning that allows artificial neural networks to benefit from a large pre-training data basis when confronted with a task of limited data. Despite its ubiquitous use and clear benefits, there are still many open questions regarding the inner workings of transfer learning and, in particular, regarding the understanding of when and how well it works. To that extent, we perform a rigorous study focusing on audio-to-audio transfer learning, in which we pre-train various model states on (ontology-based) subsets of AudioSet and fine-tune them on three computer audition tasks, namely acoustic scene recognition, bird activity recognition, and speech command recognition. We report that increasing the number of samples and classes in the pre-training data both have a positive impact on transfer learning. This is, however, generally surpassed by similarity between pre-training and the downstream task, which can lead the model to learn comparable features.

</details>


### [155] [Interpretable PM2.5 Forecasting for Urban Air Quality: A Comparative Study of Operational Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25495)
*Moazzam Umer Gondal,Hamad ul Qudous,Asma Ahmad Farhan,Sultan Alamri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三种轻量级可解释模型（SARIMAX、Facebook Prophet、NeuralProphet）在北京PM2.5小时预测中的性能，发现Facebook Prophet在周度滚动更新策略下表现最佳，而在线残差校正使SARIMAX在冻结模型策略下达到最低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空气质量预测框架通常依赖复杂、数据密集且计算需求高的模型，本研究旨在探索轻量级、可解释的预测方法是否能在北京PM2.5小时预测中提供有竞争力的性能。

Method: 采用泄漏感知预测工作流，结合时间序列数据划分、预处理、特征选择和外生驱动建模。评估了三种预测模型家族：SARIMAX、Facebook Prophet和NeuralProphet。在两种自适应机制下测试：周度滚动向前重新拟合和冻结模型在线残差校正。

Result: 在滚动重新拟合机制下，Facebook Prophet表现最佳（MAE 37.61，RMSE 50.10），且计算时间显著少于NeuralProphet。在冻结模型机制下，在线残差校正改善了Facebook Prophet和SARIMAX，校正后的SARIMAX达到最低总体误差（MAE 32.50，RMSE 46.85）。NeuralProphet在两个机制下准确性和稳定性都较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级加性预测策略在城市空气质量预测中仍具有高度竞争力，在准确性、可解释性和计算效率之间提供了实用的平衡，为实际部署提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Accurate short-term air-quality forecasting is essential for public health protection and urban management, yet many recent forecasting frameworks rely on complex, data-intensive, and computationally demanding models. This study investigates whether lightweight and interpretable forecasting approaches can provide competitive performance for hourly PM2.5 prediction in Beijing, China. Using multi-year pollutant and meteorological time-series data, we developed a leakage-aware forecasting workflow that combined chronological data partitioning, preprocessing, feature selection, and exogenous-driver modeling under the Perfect Prognosis setting. Three forecasting families were evaluated: SARIMAX, Facebook Prophet, and NeuralProphet. To assess practical deployment behavior, the models were tested under two adaptive regimes: weekly walk-forward refitting and frozen forecasting with online residual correction. Results showed clear differences in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. Under walk-forward refitting, Facebook Prophet achieved the strongest completed performance, with an MAE of $37.61$ and an RMSE of $50.10$, while also requiring substantially less execution time than NeuralProphet. In the frozen-model regime, online residual correction improved Facebook Prophet and SARIMAX, with corrected SARIMAX yielding the lowest overall error (MAE $32.50$; RMSE $46.85$). NeuralProphet remained less accurate and less stable across both regimes, and residual correction did not improve its forecasts. Notably, corrected Facebook Prophet reached nearly the same error as its walk-forward counterpart while reducing runtime from $15$ min $21.91$ sec to $46.60$ sec. These findings show that lightweight additive forecasting strategies can remain highly competitive for urban air-quality prediction, offering a practical balance between accuracy, interpretability, ...

</details>


### [156] [Revisiting On-Policy Distillation: Empirical Failure Modes and Simple Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25562)
*Yuqian Fu,Haohuan Huang,Kaiwen Jiang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了在线蒸馏(OPD)方法，指出在长序列生成任务中，基于采样token的OPD存在脆弱性问题，提出了基于教师top-K局部支持匹配的改进方法，通过截断反向KL散度和top-p采样等技术提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线蒸馏(OPD)在大语言模型后训练中具有吸引力，因为它能在学生生成的轨迹上评估教师反馈。然而在长序列生成任务中，常见的基于采样token的OPD变体存在脆弱性：它将分布匹配简化为单token信号，且随着轨迹偏离教师常见前缀而变得不可靠。

Method: 从估计器和实现两方面重新审视OPD。理论上分析token级OPD相对于序列级反向KL散度的偏差和方差权衡。提出教师top-K局部支持匹配方法，实现为带截断的反向KL散度，结合top-p轨迹采样和特殊token掩码技术。

Result: 在单任务数学推理和多任务智能体+数学训练中，提出的目标相比采样token OPD实现了更稳定的优化和更好的下游性能。玩具实验验证了未来奖励耦合越强，梯度方差越高，学习稳定性越差的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 采样token OPD存在三个失效模式：不平衡的单token信号、学生生成前缀上不可靠的教师指导、以及分词器或特殊token不匹配导致的失真。通过教师top-K局部支持匹配方法可以有效解决这些问题，提升在线蒸馏在长序列任务中的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is appealing for large language model (LLM) post-training because it evaluates teacher feedback on student-generated rollouts rather than fixed teacher traces. In long-horizon settings, however, the common sampled-token variant is fragile: it reduces distribution matching to a one-token signal and becomes increasingly unreliable as rollouts drift away from prefixes the teacher commonly visits. We revisit OPD from the estimator and implementation sides. Theoretically, token-level OPD is biased relative to sequence-level reverse-KL, but it has a much tighter worst-case variance bound; our toy study shows the same tradeoff empirically, with stronger future-reward coupling producing higher gradient variance and less stable learning. Empirically, we identify three failure modes of sampled-token OPD: an imbalanced one-token signal, unreliable teacher guidance on student-generated prefixes, and distortions caused by tokenizer or special-token mismatch. We address these issues with teacher top-K local support matching, implemented as truncated reverse-KL with top-p rollout sampling and special-token masking. Across single-task math reasoning and multi-task agentic-plus-math training, this objective yields more stable optimization and better downstream performance than sampled-token OPD.

</details>


### [157] [Uncertainty-Guided Label Rebalancing for CPS Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25670)
*John Ayotunde,Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel C. Briand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: U-Balance：一种利用行为不确定性重新平衡CPS安全监测中极端类别不平衡数据集的监督方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界CPS操作中不安全事件罕见，导致极端类别不平衡，降低安全预测器性能；标准重平衡技术对时间序列CPS遥测数据效果不佳；行为不确定性常与安全结果相关但未在安全监测中探索

Method: 1. 训练基于GatedMLP的不确定性预测器，将遥测窗口总结为分布运动学特征并输出不确定性分数；2. 应用不确定性引导标签重平衡机制，将具有异常高不确定性的安全标签窗口概率性地重新标记为不安全；3. 在重平衡数据集上训练安全预测器进行安全监测

Result: 在46:1安全-不安全比例的大规模无人机基准测试中，U-Balance达到0.806 F1分数，比最强基线提高14.3个百分点；确认行为不确定性与安全存在中度但显著相关性；uLNR被证明是利用不确定性信息的最有效策略

Conclusion: U-Balance通过利用行为不确定性重新平衡数据集，有效解决了CPS安全监测中的极端类别不平衡问题，显著提升了安全预测性能，同时保持了有竞争力的推理效率

Abstract: Safety monitoring is essential for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). However, unsafe events are rare in real-world CPS operations, creating an extreme class imbalance that degrades safety predictors. Standard rebalancing techniques perform poorly on time-series CPS telemetry, either generating unrealistic synthetic samples or overfitting on the minority class. Meanwhile, behavioral uncertainty in CPS operations, defined as the degree of doubt or uncertainty in CPS decisions , is often correlated with safety outcomes but unexplored in safety monitoring. To that end, we propose U-Balance, a supervised approach that leverages behavioral uncertainty to rebalance imbalanced datasets prior to training a safety predictor. U-Balance first trains a GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor that summarizes each telemetry window into distributional kinematic features and outputs an uncertainty score. It then applies an uncertainty-guided label rebalancing (uLNR) mechanism that probabilistically relabels \textit{safe}-labeled windows with unusually high uncertainty as \textit{unsafe}, thereby enriching the minority class with informative boundary samples without synthesizing new data. Finally, a safety predictor is trained on the rebalanced dataset for safety monitoring. We evaluate U-Balance on a large-scale UAV benchmark with a 46:1 safe-to-unsafe ratio. Results confirm a moderate but significant correlation between behavioral uncertainty and safety. We then identify uLNR as the most effective strategy to exploit uncertainty information, compared to direct early and late fusion. U-Balance achieves a 0.806 F1 score, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.3 percentage points, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency. Ablation studies confirm that both the GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor and the uLNR mechanism contribute significantly to U-Balance's effectiveness.

</details>


### [158] [A Unified Memory Perspective for Probabilistic Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25692)
*Xueji Zhao,Likai Pei,Jianbo Liu,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将确定性数据访问视为随机采样的特例，建立统一的数据访问框架，揭示随机性需求降低数据访问效率并导致熵受限操作，提出内存级评估标准，分析传统架构局限和新兴概率存内计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信人工智能日益依赖概率计算实现鲁棒性、可解释性、安全性和隐私保护。实际系统中，这些工作负载在确定性数据访问和跨模型、数据路径及系统功能的重复随机采样之间交错，将性能瓶颈从算术单元转移到必须同时提供数据和随机性的内存系统。

Method: 提出统一的数据访问视角，将确定性访问视为随机采样的极限情况，使两种模式能在共同框架下分析。基于此视角定义内存级评估标准：统一操作、分布可编程性、效率、对硬件非理想性的鲁棒性以及并行兼容性。

Result: 该视角揭示增加随机性需求会降低有效数据访问效率，并可能驱动系统进入熵受限操作。使用这些标准分析了传统架构的局限性，并研究了新兴的概率存内计算方法，这些方法将采样与内存访问集成。

Conclusion: 通过统一的数据访问框架和评估标准，为可信AI的可扩展硬件设计提供了途径，特别是通过概率存内计算方法来应对随机性需求带来的内存系统挑战。

Abstract: Trustworthy artificial intelligence increasingly relies on probabilistic computation to achieve robustness, interpretability, security and privacy. In practical systems, such workloads interleave deterministic data access with repeated stochastic sampling across models, data paths and system functions, shifting performance bottlenecks from arithmetic units to memory systems that must deliver both data and randomness. Here we present a unified data-access perspective in which deterministic access is treated as a limiting case of stochastic sampling, enabling both modes to be analyzed within a common framework. This view reveals that increasing stochastic demand reduces effective data-access efficiency and can drive systems into entropy-limited operation. Based on this insight, we define memory-level evaluation criteria, including unified operation, distribution programmability, efficiency, robustness to hardware non-idealities and parallel compatibility. Using these criteria, we analyze limitations of conventional architectures and examine emerging probabilistic compute-in-memory approaches that integrate sampling with memory access, outlining pathways toward scalable hardware for trustworthy AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Saranga: MilliWatt Ultrasound for Navigation in Visually Degraded Environments on Palm-Sized Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24699)
*Manoj Velmurugan,Phillip Brush,Colin Balfour,Richard J. Przybyla,Nitin J. Sanket*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Saranga：一种基于超声波的感知系统，使微型无人机能够在视觉退化条件下（如浓雾、黑暗、雪天）导航，通过双声纳阵列和深度学习降噪解决低信噪比问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机在受限和杂乱环境中具有出色的敏捷性和成本效益，但其有限的负载能力限制了机载传感套件，特别是在GPS拒止的野外场景中。传统基于摄像头和激光雷达的避障方法在视觉退化条件下（低能见度、灰尘、雾、黑暗）失效，而雷达等高功耗传感器不适合微型无人机。受蝙蝠启发，需要开发低功耗的超声波感知方案。

Method: 提出Saranga低功耗超声波感知系统：1）使用双声纳阵列进行障碍物定位；2）采用物理降噪方法阻挡螺旋桨引起的超声波噪声；3）开发基于深度学习的降噪方法，利用超声波回声的长时域模式在高度不相关噪声中识别信号模式；4）通过合成数据生成管道和有限真实噪声数据进行训练，实现现实世界泛化。

Result: 系统在信噪比低至-4.9分贝的条件下有效工作。实现了掌上型无人机在浓雾、黑暗和雪天等视觉退化条件下，在包含薄型和透明障碍物的杂乱环境中导航，仅使用机载传感和计算。提供了广泛的真实世界结果证明方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Saranga系统成功解决了微型无人机在视觉退化条件下的导航挑战，通过创新的物理降噪和深度学习降噪方法克服了超声波感知中的低信噪比问题，为微型无人机在恶劣环境中的自主导航提供了可行的低功耗解决方案。

Abstract: Tiny palm-sized aerial robots possess exceptional agility and cost-effectiveness in navigating confined and cluttered environments. However, their limited payload capacity directly constrains the sensing suite on-board the robot, thereby limiting critical navigational tasks in Global Positioning System (GPS)-denied wild scenes. Common methods for obstacle avoidance use cameras and LIght Detection And Ranging (LIDAR), which become ineffective in visually degraded conditions such as low visibility, dust, fog or darkness. Other sensors, such as RAdio Detection And Ranging (RADAR), have high power consumption, making them unsuitable for tiny aerial robots. Inspired by bats, we propose Saranga, a low-power ultrasound-based perception stack that localizes obstacles using a dual sonar array. We present two key solutions to combat the low Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio of $-4.9$ decibels: physical noise reduction and a deep learning based denoising method. Firstly, we present a practical way to block propeller induced ultrasound noise on the weak echoes. The second solution is to train a neural network to utilize the \textcolor{black}{long horizon of ultrasound echoes} for finding signal patterns under high amounts of uncorrelated noise where classical methods were insufficient. We generalize to the real world by using a synthetic data generation pipeline and limited real noise data for training. We enable a palm-sized aerial robot to navigate in visually degraded conditions of dense fog, darkness, and snow in a cluttered environment with thin and transparent obstacles using only on-board sensing and computation. We provide extensive real world results to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [160] [FODMP: Fast One-Step Diffusion of Movement Primitives Generation for Time-Dependent Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24806)
*Xirui Shi,Arya Ebrahimi,Yi Hu,Jun Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FODMP：一种快速单步扩散运动基元框架，通过一致性蒸馏将扩散模型转化为ProDMPs轨迹参数空间，实现高速推理和时序结构化运动生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习中的扩散模型存在明显权衡：动作分块扩散策略（如ManiCM）推理快但只能预测短运动片段，缺乏时间依赖性运动基元；运动基元扩散（MPD）虽能生成时序结构化运动，但多步扩散过程导致推理延迟过高，无法满足实时控制需求

Method: 提出FODMP框架，将扩散模型蒸馏到概率动态运动基元（ProDMPs）的轨迹参数空间中，使用单步解码器生成运动。通过一致性蒸馏消除推理瓶颈，保留运动基元的时间结构特性

Result: 在标准操作基准测试（MetaWorld、ManiSkill）中，FODMP比MPD快10倍，比动作分块扩散策略快7倍，同时达到或超过其成功率。能够生成快速加速-减速运动基元，实现实时拦截快速飞行球的任务

Conclusion: FODMP成功解决了扩散模型在机器人学习中的速度-能力权衡问题，实现了高速推理与时间依赖性运动基元的结合，适用于闭环视觉控制场景

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used for robot learning, but current designs face a clear trade-off. Action-chunking diffusion policies like ManiCM are fast to run, yet they only predict short segments of motion. This makes them reactive, but unable to capture time-dependent motion primitives, such as following a spring-damper-like behavior with built-in dynamic profiles of acceleration and deceleration. Recently, Movement Primitive Diffusion (MPD) partially addresses this limitation by parameterizing full trajectories using Probabilistic Dynamic Movement Primitives (ProDMPs), thereby enabling the generation of temporally structured motions. Nevertheless, MPD integrates the motion decoder directly into a multi-step diffusion process, resulting in prohibitively high inference latency that limits its applicability in real-time control settings. We propose FODMP (Fast One-step Diffusion of Movement Primitives), a new framework that distills diffusion models into the ProDMPs trajectory parameter space and generates motion using a single-step decoder. FODMP retains the temporal structure of movement primitives while eliminating the inference bottleneck through single-step consistency distillation. This enables robots to execute time-dependent primitives at high inference speed, suitable for closed-loop vision-based control. On standard manipulation benchmarks (MetaWorld, ManiSkill), FODMP runs up to 10 times faster than MPD and 7 times faster than action-chunking diffusion policies, while matching or exceeding their success rates. Beyond speed, by generating fast acceleration-deceleration motion primitives, FODMP allows the robot to intercept and securely catch a fast-flying ball, whereas action-chunking diffusion policy and MPD respond too slowly for real-time interception.

</details>


### [161] [Characterization of Constraints in Flexible Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24813)
*Samrat Bhattacharyya,Nabil Simaan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线路径规划算法，用于在未知环境中安全自主操作柔性约束物体，通过实时识别和表征感知到的柔性约束及全局刚度，实现同时探索、表征和操作弹性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中安全操作柔性约束物体（如手术中的器官牵拉）需要实时识别约束特性，以避免损伤并实现精确控制。现有方法缺乏对柔性约束的实时表征能力。

Method: 基于局部力和位置信息的约束探索方法，在物体轨迹的多个位姿处考虑感知约束刚度，利用刚度特征向量信息，通过简单机械约束（如铰链、平面约束）的图谱来表征和识别全局刚度行为。

Result: 通过仿真和实验验证了算法能够实时识别多种常见简单机械约束（如柔性铰链），并识别相关螺旋参数，证明了同时进行全局约束/刚度探索和安全操作柔性约束物体的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为实现安全协作操作（如手术中的器官牵拉和操作）奠定了基础，展示了同时探索、表征和操作柔性约束系统的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an online path planning algorithm for safe autonomous manipulation of a flexibly constrained object in an unknown environment. Methods for real time identification and characterization of perceived flexible constraints and global stiffness are presented. Used in tandem, these methods allow a robot to simultaneously explore, characterize, and manipulate an elastic system safely. Navigation without a-priori knowledge of the system is achieved using constraint exploration based on local force and position information. The perceived constraint stiffness is considered at multiple poses along an object's (system) trajectory. Using stiffness eigenvector information, global stiffness behavior is characterized and identified using an atlas of simple mechanical constraints, such as hinges and planar constraints. Validation of these algorithms is carried out by simulation and experimentally. The ability to recognize several common simple mechanical constraints (such as a flexible hinge) in real time, and to subsequently identify relevant screw parameters is demonstrated. These results suggest the feasibility of simultaneous global constrain/stiffness exploration and safe manipulation of flexibly constrained objects. We believe that this approach will eventually enable safe cooperative manipulation in applications such as organ retraction and manipulation during surgery

</details>


### [162] [Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation, Sequencing, and Optimal Trajectory Generation in Obstacle-Rich 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24908)
*Yunes Alqudsi,Murat Makaraci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMD-TAPP是一个端到端框架，用于协调多无人机在复杂3D环境中的任务分配和路径规划，通过图搜索、粒子群优化和时间参数化轨迹生成，实现动态可行且无碰撞的协同任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂三维环境中协调多无人机团队需要将离散的任务规划（决定哪个无人机服务哪个目标以及顺序）与连续时间轨迹合成（确保避碰和动态可行性）进行原理性集成。现有方法往往分别处理这些问题，缺乏端到端的协同解决方案。

Method: 1. 将工作空间离散化为3D导航图，通过图搜索计算机器人到目标和目标到目标的障碍感知旅行成本；2. 使用注入粒子群优化（IPSO）方案，结合多线性分配，探索耦合的任务分配/排序方案以最小化任务完成时间；3. 将生成的航点路径通过生成-优化例程转换为时间参数化的最小抖动轨迹，迭代验证障碍物间隙和机器人间距离，在安全裕度被违反时触发重新规划。

Result: 在复杂的3D场景中进行广泛的MATLAB仿真表明，IMD-TAPP始终能生成动态可行且无碰撞的轨迹，同时实现具有竞争力的完成时间。在一个代表性案例研究中，两架无人机服务多个目标，该方法实现了136秒的最小任务时间，并在整个执行过程中保持了所需的安全约束。

Conclusion: IMD-TAPP提供了一个有效的端到端框架，用于多无人机在复杂3D环境中的协同任务执行，成功整合了任务分配、路径规划和轨迹生成，确保了动态可行性和安全性，在任务完成时间方面表现出色。

Abstract: Coordinating teams of aerial robots in cluttered three-dimensional (3D) environments requires a principled integration of discrete mission planning-deciding which robot serves which goals and in what order -- with continuous-time trajectory synthesis that enforces collision avoidance and dynamic feasibility. This paper introduces IMD-TAPP (Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Path Planning), an end-to-end framework that jointly addresses multi-goal allocation, tour sequencing, and safe trajectory generation for quadrotor teams operating in obstacle-rich spaces. IMD--TAPP first discretizes the workspace into a 3D navigation graph and computes obstacle-aware robot-to-goal and goal-to-goal travel costs via graph-search-based pathfinding. These costs are then embedded within an Injected Particle Swarm Optimization (IPSO) scheme, guided by multiple linear assignment, to efficiently explore coupled assignment/ordering alternatives and to minimize mission makespan. Finally, the resulting waypoint tours are transformed into time-parameterized minimum-snap trajectories through a generation-and-optimization routine equipped with iterative validation of obstacle clearance and inter-robot separation, triggering re-planning when safety margins are violated. Extensive MATLAB simulations across cluttered 3D scenarios demonstrate that IMD--TAPP consistently produces dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories while achieving competitive completion times. In a representative case study with two drones serving multiple goals, the proposed approach attains a minimum mission time of 136~s while maintaining the required safety constraints throughout execution.

</details>


### [163] [CROSS: A Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning Framework for Generalizable Large-Scale Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24930)
*Xibei Chen,Yifeng Zhang,Yuxiang Xiao,Mingfeng Fan,Maonan Wang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CROSS：基于混合专家（MoE）的去中心化强化学习框架，用于可泛化的自适应交通信号控制，通过预测对比聚类和场景自适应MoE模块提升对多样化交通模式的表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自适应交通信号控制方法通常对所有场景使用单一共享策略，其有限表征能力难以捕捉多样化的交通动态并泛化到未见环境，需要解决大规模ATSC的有效性和泛化性挑战。

Method: 提出CROSS框架：1) 预测对比聚类（PCC）模块，通过预测短期状态转移来识别潜在交通模式，结合聚类和对比学习增强模式级表征；2) 场景自适应MoE模块，用多个专家增强共享策略，实现自适应专业化和更灵活的场景特定策略。

Result: 在SUMO模拟器上对合成和真实世界交通数据集进行广泛实验，与最先进基线相比，CROSS通过改进对多样化交通场景的表征，实现了优越的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CROSS框架通过结合预测对比聚类和场景自适应混合专家模块，有效解决了自适应交通信号控制的泛化挑战，为未来智慧城市中的大规模交通系统优化提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics, automation, and artificial intelligence have enabled urban traffic systems to operate with increasing autonomy towards future smart cities, powered in part by the development of adaptive traffic signal control (ATSC), which dynamically optimizes signal phases to mitigate congestion and optimize traffic. However, achieving effective and generalizable large-scale ATSC remains a significant challenge due to the diverse intersection topologies and highly dynamic, complex traffic demand patterns across the network. Existing RL-based methods typically use a single shared policy for all scenarios, whose limited representational capacity makes it difficult to capture diverse traffic dynamics and generalize to unseen environments. To address these challenges, we propose CROSS, a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based decentralized RL framework for generalizable ATSC. We first introduce a Predictive Contrastive Clustering (PCC) module that forecasts short-term state transitions to identify latent traffic patterns, followed by clustering and contrastive learning to enhance pattern-level representation. We further design a Scenario-Adaptive MoE module that augments a shared policy with multiple experts, thus enabling adaptive specialization and more flexible scenario-specific strategies. We conduct extensive experiments in the SUMO simulator on both synthetic and real-world traffic datasets. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, CROSS achieves superior performance and generalization through improved representation of diverse traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [164] [COIN: Collaborative Interaction-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Self-Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24931)
*Yifeng Zhang,Jieming Chen,Tingguang Zhou,Tanishq Duhan,Jianghong Dong,Yuhong Cao,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COIN是一个用于多智能体自动驾驶系统的协作交互感知MARL框架，通过CIG-TD3算法和双层交互感知中心化评论家架构，在密集城市交通环境中实现安全高效的协作驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自动驾驶系统能协调自动驾驶车辆减少拥堵、提高安全和效率，但在密集场景中实现高效安全的协作仍面临挑战，特别是复杂的智能体交互问题。

Method: 提出COIN框架，包含CIG-TD3算法（基于CTDE范式），联合优化个体导航目标和全局协作目标；采用双层交互感知中心化评论家架构，捕捉局部成对交互和全局系统级依赖。

Result: 在密集城市交通环境中的仿真实验表明，COIN在各种系统规模下均优于其他先进基线方法，在安全和效率方面表现更优，并通过真实机器人演示验证。

Conclusion: COIN框架能有效解决密集多智能体自动驾驶系统中的协作挑战，通过创新的算法和架构设计实现了更准确的价值估计和信用分配，在复杂动态场景中具有优越性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Self-Driving (MASD) systems provide an effective solution for coordinating autonomous vehicles to reduce congestion and enhance both safety and operational efficiency in future intelligent transportation systems. Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has emerged as a promising approach for developing advanced end-to-end MASD systems. However, achieving efficient and safe collaboration in dynamic MASD systems remains a significant challenge in dense scenarios with complex agent interactions. To address this challenge, we propose a novel collaborative(CO-) interaction-aware(-IN) MARL framework, named COIN. Specifically, we develop a new counterfactual individual-global twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient (CIG-TD3) algorithm, crafted in a "centralized training, decentralized execution" (CTDE) manner, which aims to jointly optimize the individual objectives (navigation) and the global objectives (collaboration) of agents. We further introduce a dual-level interaction-aware centralized critic architecture that captures both local pairwise interactions and global system-level dependencies, enabling more accurate global value estimation and improved credit assignment for collaborative policy learning. We conduct extensive simulation experiments in dense urban traffic environments, which demonstrate that COIN consistently outperforms other advanced baseline methods in both safety and efficiency across various system sizes. These results highlight its superiority in complex and dynamic MASD scenarios, as further validated through real-world robot demonstrations. Supplementary videos are available at https://marmotlab.github.io/COIN/

</details>


### [165] [Wireless bioelectronics for untethered biohybrid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24959)
*Hiroyuki Tetsuka,Minoru Hirano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该视角文章总结了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展，包括无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合，并提出了未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物混合机器人面临的主要挑战是如何在不依赖有线连接或浸入细胞培养基的笨重硬件的情况下，传递刺激和控制信号。无线生物电子学和光电子学为解决这一限制提供了可能。

Method: 文章采用视角综述的方法，总结并分析了三种主要控制策略：1) 无线电刺激通过射频磁场远程传递控制信号；2) 无线光刺激通过嵌入式光源远程控制光遗传学修饰的肌肉；3) 神经肌肉整合利用生物信号转导实现多执行器的选择性控制。

Result: 文章系统总结了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展，提出了跨领域设计原则，并展示了无线控制技术如何克服传统有线系统的局限性。

Conclusion: 未来发展方向是神经类器官-生物电子学的协同整合，以实现自主、闭环的生物混合机器人系统。

Abstract: Biohybrid robots integrate living tissues with engineered artificial structures to achieve organism-inspired actuation and behavior. A persistent challenge is delivering stimulation and control signals without relying on tethered wiring or bulky hardware immersed in cell-culture media. Wireless bioelectronics addresses this limitation by enabling the remote transfer of control signals, typically via radio-frequency magnetic fields, to locally stimulate muscle tissues at tissue-electrode interfaces. In parallel, wireless optoelectronics enables remote control of optogenetically modified, muscle-based robots by embedding light emitters that initiate muscle actuation through light-gated ion channels. Further advances incorporate neuromuscular junctions, leveraging biological signal transduction to enable selective control of multiple actuators through wireless frequency- and time-division multiplexing. This perspective article summarizes recent advances in control strategies for biohybrid robots, namely, wireless electrical stimulation, wireless optical stimulation, and neuromuscular integration. Then this describes cross-cutting design principles and highlights a future direction, namely, co-integration of neural organoid-bioelectronics toward autonomous, closed-loop biohybrid robots.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Rollout from Sampling:An R1-Style Tokenized Traffic Simulation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24989)
*Ziyan Wang,Peng Chen,Ding Li,Chiwei Li,Qichao Zhang,Zhongpu Xia,Guizhen Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R1Sim：基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习交通仿真方法，通过熵引导自适应采样和组相对策略优化实现平衡的探索-利用权衡，生成真实、安全、多样的多智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于下一令牌预测（NTP）范式的交通仿真方法限制了在次优区域对潜在有价值运动令牌的主动探索。熵模式为基于运动令牌不确定性的探索提供了有前景的视角。

Method: 提出R1Sim令牌化交通仿真策略：1）熵引导自适应采样机制，关注高不确定性但高潜力的运动令牌；2）基于安全感知奖励设计的组相对策略优化（GRPO）优化运动行为；3）通过多样高不确定性采样和组间比较估计实现探索-利用平衡。

Result: 在Waymo Sim Agent基准测试中，R1Sim相比最先进方法实现了竞争性性能，能够生成真实、安全、多样的多智能体行为。

Conclusion: R1Sim是基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习交通仿真的首次尝试，通过熵引导采样和GRPO优化，有效解决了现有NTP方法在探索方面的局限性，实现了更好的仿真效果。

Abstract: Learning diverse and high-fidelity traffic simulations from human driving demonstrations is crucial for autonomous driving evaluation. The recent next-token prediction (NTP) paradigm, widely adopted in large language models (LLMs), has been applied to traffic simulation and achieves iterative improvements via supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, such methods limit active exploration of potentially valuable motion tokens, particularly in suboptimal regions. Entropy patterns provide a promising perspective for enabling exploration driven by motion token uncertainty. Motivated by this insight, we propose a novel tokenized traffic simulation policy, R1Sim, which represents an initial attempt to explore reinforcement learning based on motion token entropy patterns, and systematically analyzes the impact of different motion tokens on simulation outcomes. Specifically, we introduce an entropy-guided adaptive sampling mechanism that focuses on previously overlooked motion tokens with high uncertainty yet high potential. We further optimize motion behaviors using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), guided by a safety-aware reward design. Overall, these components enable a balanced exploration-exploitation trade-off through diverse high-uncertainty sampling and group-wise comparative estimation, resulting in realistic, safe, and diverse multi-agent behaviors. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Sim Agent benchmark demonstrate that R1Sim achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [167] [$π$, But Make It Fly: Physics-Guided Transfer of VLA Models to Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25038)
*Johnathan Tucker,Denis Liu,Aiden Swann,Allen Ren,Javier Yu,Jiankai Sun,Brandon Kim,Lachlain McGranahan,Quan Vuong,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirVLA系统研究将预训练的固定基座机械臂视觉-语言-动作模型迁移到空中拾取放置任务，通过载荷感知引导机制和高斯溅射合成数据解决动力学差异和数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLA模型在固定基座机械臂上表现出色，但将其迁移到空中平台面临挑战，因为固定基座机械臂的准静态动力学与飞行的欠驱动、高动态特性存在根本性不匹配。

Method: 引入AirVLA系统，包含：1）载荷感知引导机制，将载荷约束直接注入策略的流匹配采样过程；2）使用高斯溅射管道合成导航训练数据以克服数据稀缺问题。

Result: 460次真实世界实验显示：合成数据使导航任务成功率从81%提升至100%；载荷感知引导将拾取放置任务成功率从23%提升至50%；长时域组合任务总体成功率达62%。

Conclusion: 预训练的机械臂VLA模型通过适当的数据增强和物理信息引导，可以迁移到空中操纵和导航任务，以及这些任务的组合，为空中平台的基础模型应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models such as $π_0$ have demonstrated remarkable generalization across diverse fixed-base manipulators. However, transferring these foundation models to aerial platforms remains an open challenge due to the fundamental mismatch between the quasi-static dynamics of fixed-base arms and the underactuated, highly dynamic nature of flight. In this work, we introduce AirVLA, a system that investigates the transferability of manipulation-pretrained VLAs to aerial pick-and-place tasks. We find that while visual representations transfer effectively, the specific control dynamics required for flight do not. To bridge this "dynamics gap" without retraining the foundation model, we introduce a Payload-Aware Guidance mechanism that injects payload constraints directly into the policy's flow-matching sampling process. To overcome data scarcity, we further utilize a Gaussian Splatting pipeline to synthesize navigation training data. We evaluate our method through a cumulative 460 real-world experiments which demonstrate that this synthetic data is a key enabler of performance, unlocking 100% success in navigation tasks where directly fine-tuning on teleoperation data alone attains 81% success. Our inference-time intervention, Payload-Aware Guidance, increases real-world pick-and-place task success from 23% to 50%. Finally, we evaluate the model on a long-horizon compositional task, achieving a 62% overall success rate. These results suggest that pre-trained manipulation VLAs, with appropriate data augmentation and physics-informed guidance, can transfer to aerial manipulation and navigation, as well as the composition of these tasks.

</details>


### [168] [ThermoAct:Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Perception and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25044)
*Young-Chae Son,Dae-Kwan Ko,Yoon-Ji Choi,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合热信息的视觉-语言-动作框架，提升机器人任务执行的安全性和智能性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作环境中，传感器数据融合多局限于视觉信息，热数据对机器人安全和操作效率至关重要但被忽视，需要整合热信息以增强机器人对物理属性的感知和环境安全

Method: 提出新颖的视觉-语言-动作框架，利用视觉语言模型作为高层规划器解析复杂自然语言指令并分解为子任务，集成热信息使机器人能感知物理属性并主动确保环境安全

Result: 真实世界任务场景的实验验证了框架的可行性，相比现有视觉系统，能提升任务成功率和安全性

Conclusion: 融合热信息的视觉-语言-动作框架为机器人任务执行提供了更安全、更智能的解决方案，具有实际应用潜力

Abstract: In recent human-robot collaboration environments, there is a growing focus on integrating diverse sensor data beyond visual information to enable safer and more intelligent task execution. Although thermal data can be crucial for enhancing robot safety and operational efficiency, its integration has been relatively overlooked in prior research. This paper proposes a novel Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that incorporates thermal information for robot task execution. The proposed system leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a high-level planner to interpret complex natural language commands and decompose them into simpler sub-tasks. This approach facilitates efficient data collection and robust reasoning for complex operations. Unlike conventional methods that rely solely on visual data, our approach integrates thermal information, enabling the robot to perceive physical properties and proactively ensure environmental safety. Experimental results from real-world task scenarios validate the feasibility of our proposed framework, suggesting its potential to enhance task success rates and safety compared to existing vision-based systems.

</details>


### [169] [CTS-PLL: A Robust and Anytime Framework for Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25121)
*Junkai Jiang,Yitao Xu,Ruochen Li,Shaobing Xu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CTS-PLL：一种用于协作任务排序和多智能体路径规划的分层框架，通过锁智能体检测与释放机制和基于大邻域搜索的随时优化，在密集环境中实现高成功率和解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS-MAPF问题要求智能体在避免碰撞的同时完成任务序列，其组合复杂性带来重大挑战。现有方法在密集环境中鲁棒性不足，且缺乏持续优化解质量的能力。

Method: 扩展基于配置的CTS-MAPF规划范式，引入两个关键增强：1) 利用完整规划方法进行局部重规划的锁智能体检测与释放机制；2) 基于大邻域搜索(LNS)的随时优化过程。

Result: 在稀疏和密集基准测试中，CTS-PLL相比现有方法实现了更高的成功率和解质量，同时保持了有竞争力的运行时效率。真实机器人实验进一步验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: CTS-PLL框架通过分层设计和优化机制，有效解决了CTS-MAPF问题在密集环境中的鲁棒性和解质量优化问题，为实际机器人应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding (CTS-MAPF) problem requires agents to accomplish sequences of tasks while avoiding collisions, posing significant challenges due to its combinatorial complexity. This work introduces CTS-PLL, a hierarchical framework that extends the configuration-based CTS-MAPF planning paradigm with two key enhancements: a lock agents detection and release mechanism leveraging a complete planning method for local re-planning, and an anytime refinement procedure based on Large Neighborhood Search (LNS). These additions ensure robustness in dense environments and enable continuous improvement of solution quality. Extensive evaluations across sparse and dense benchmarks demonstrate that CTS-PLL achieves higher success rates and solution quality compared with existing methods, while maintaining competitive runtime efficiency. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of the approach in practice.

</details>


### [170] [A Minimum-Energy Control Approach for Redundant Mobile Manipulators in Physical Human-Robot Interaction Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25259)
*Davide Tebaldi,Niccolò Paradisi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于移动机械臂的控制方法，旨在最小化人机物理交互中整个系统的动能，并通过孔轴装配任务验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂相比固定基座机械臂具有更多自由度，在人机物理交互任务中控制设计更具挑战性，需要优化系统性能。

Method: 提出一种控制方法，针对由移动基座和顶部安装机械臂组成的移动机械臂系统，目标是最小化整个系统在物理人机交互中的动能。

Result: 通过孔轴装配任务的实验验证，该方法减少了整个机器人系统的动能，相比基准方法提高了系统性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制方法能有效优化移动机械臂在人机物理交互中的性能，通过动能最小化改善了系统表现。

Abstract: Research on mobile manipulation systems that physically interact with humans has expanded rapidly in recent years, opening the way to tasks which could not be performed using fixed-base manipulators. Within this context, developing suitable control methodologies is essential since mobile manipulators introduce additional degrees of freedom, making the design of control approaches more challenging and more prone to performance optimization. This paper proposes a control approach for a mobile manipulator, composed of a mobile base equipped with a robotic arm mounted on the top, with the objective of minimizing the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body mobile manipulator in physical human-robot interaction applications. The approach is experimentally tested with reference to a peg-in-hole task, and the results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body robotic system and improves the system performance compared with the benchmark method.

</details>


### [171] [Connectivity-Aware Representations for Constrained Motion Planning via Multi-Scale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25298)
*Suhyun Jeon,Yumin Lim,Woo-Jeong Baek,Hyeonseo Kim,Suhan Park,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种学习连通性感知表示的方法，用于在运动规划前选择起点和终点配置，通过多尺度流形学习和对比学习框架来避免本质上互不连通组件的问题，从而提高规划成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划面临三个主要挑战：1）起点和终点配置可能位于互不连通的区域（本质上互不连通组件）；2）约束将可行空间限制到低维子流形；3）冗余性引入额外复杂性，因为单个末端执行器姿态可能有无限多逆运动学解，形成离散的自运动流形。这些问题导致运动规划效率低下甚至不可行。

Method: 提出学习连通性感知表示的方法：1）通过多尺度流形学习将关节配置嵌入到潜在空间，覆盖从局部到全局的邻域范围；2）通过聚类生成伪标签；3）使用对比学习框架进行监督学习。该框架提供连通性感知度量，用于偏向选择连通区域中的起点和终点配置。

Result: 在各种操作任务上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法实现了1.9倍更高的成功率，并将规划时间减少了0.43倍（即减少了57%的规划时间）。

Conclusion: 通过学习连通性感知表示来选择起点和终点配置，可以有效避免本质上互不连通组件的问题，显著提高约束运动规划的成功率和效率，为解决复杂约束下的运动规划问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The objective of constrained motion planning is to connect start and goal configurations while satisfying task-specific constraints. Motion planning becomes inefficient or infeasible when the configurations lie in disconnected regions, known as essentially mutually disconnected (EMD) components. Constraints further restrict feasible space to a lower-dimensional submanifold, while redundancy introduces additional complexity because a single end-effector pose admits infinitely many inverse kinematic solutions that may form discrete self-motion manifolds. This paper addresses these challenges by learning a connectivity-aware representation for selecting start and goal configurations prior to planning. Joint configurations are embedded into a latent space through multi-scale manifold learning across neighborhood ranges from local to global, and clustering generates pseudo-labels that supervise a contrastive learning framework. The proposed framework provides a connectivity-aware measure that biases the selection of start and goal configurations in connected regions, avoiding EMDs and yielding higher success rates with reduced planning time. Experiments on various manipulation tasks showed that our method achieves 1.9 times higher success rates and reduces the planning time by a factor of 0.43 compared to baselines.

</details>


### [172] [SafeGuard ASF: SR Agentic Humanoid Robot System for Autonomous Industrial Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25353)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Tuan Tran,Ben Wei Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeGuard ASF是一个部署人形机器人进行工业危险自主检测的框架，整合多模态感知、ReAct智能体推理和学习的运动策略，针对火灾烟雾、管道异常温度和入侵者检测三种危险场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人"黑灯工厂"的兴起，需要能够检测和响应多种危险类型的自主安全系统，以在没有人类在场的情况下确保工业环境安全。

Method: 采用Unitree G1人形机器人平台，整合RGB-D多模态感知、基于ReAct的智能体推理框架和学习的运动策略。感知流水线用于危险检测，使用Unitree RL Lab和PPO算法训练多种运动策略，ToolOrchestra行动框架实现结构化决策。

Result: 火灾烟雾检测达到94.2% mAP，延迟127ms；运动策略在80,000次训练迭代内稳定收敛；系统在仿真和真实环境中验证了自主巡逻、人类检测和避障能力。

Conclusion: SafeGuard ASF框架成功展示了人形机器人在工业危险检测中的可行性，通过整合感知、推理和行动能力，为无人工厂的自主安全系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The rise of unmanned ``dark factories'' operating without human presence demands autonomous safety systems capable of detecting and responding to multiple hazard types. We present SafeGuard ASF (Agentic Security Fleet), a comprehensive framework deploying humanoid robots for autonomous hazard detection in industrial environments. Our system integrates multi-modal perception (RGB-D imaging), a ReAct-based agentic reasoning framework, and learned locomotion policies on the Unitree G1 humanoid platform. We address three critical hazard scenarios: fire and smoke detection, abnormal temperature monitoring in pipelines, and intruder detection in restricted zones. Our perception pipeline achieves 94.2% mAP for fire or smoke detection with 127ms latency. We train multiple locomotion policies, including dance motion tracking and velocity control, using Unitree RL Lab with PPO, demonstrating stable convergence within 80,000 training iterations. We validate our system in both simulation and real-world environments, demonstrating autonomous patrol, human detection with visual perception, and obstacle avoidance capabilities. The proposed ToolOrchestra action framework enables structured decision-making through perception, reasoning, and actuation tools.

</details>


### [173] [Integrating Deep RL and Bayesian Inference for ObjectNav in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25366)
*João Castelo-Branco,José Santos-Victor,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合贝叶斯推理与深度强化学习的混合物体搜索框架，在室内环境中实现更高效可靠的物体搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在室内环境中进行自主物体搜索面临部分可观测性、感知不确定性和探索-导航效率权衡等挑战。传统概率方法依赖人工设计的动作选择启发式，而深度强化学习虽然能自适应但收敛慢且可解释性有限

Method: 提出混合框架：通过贝叶斯推理维护目标位置的空间置信度图，从校准的物体检测中在线更新；训练强化学习策略直接从该概率表示中选择导航动作

Result: 在Habitat 3.0的室内仿真环境中评估，相比基准策略，在两个室内环境中提高了成功率并减少了搜索工作量

Conclusion: 贝叶斯置信度估计与学习动作选择的结合在部分可观测条件下能实现更高效可靠的物体搜索行为

Abstract: Autonomous object search is challenging for mobile robots operating in indoor environments due to partial observability, perceptual uncertainty, and the need to trade off exploration and navigation efficiency. Classical probabilistic approaches explicitly represent uncertainty but typically rely on handcrafted action-selection heuristics, while deep reinforcement learning enables adaptive policies but often suffers from slow convergence and limited interpretability. This paper proposes a hybrid object-search framework that integrates Bayesian inference with deep reinforcement learning. The method maintains a spatial belief map over target locations, updated online through Bayesian inference from calibrated object detections, and trains a reinforcement learning policy to select navigation actions directly from this probabilistic representation. The approach is evaluated in realistic indoor simulation using Habitat 3.0 and compared against developed baseline strategies. Across two indoor environments, the proposed method improves success rate while reducing search effort. Overall, the results support the value of combining Bayesian belief estimation with learned action selection to achieve more efficient and reliable objectsearch behavior under partial observability.

</details>


### [174] [IntentReact: Guiding Reactive Object-Centric Navigation via Topological Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25382)
*Yanmei Jiao,Anpeng Lu,Wenhan Hu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Huajin Tang,Wen-an Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IntentReact：一种意图条件化的物体中心导航框架，通过将全局拓扑规划编码为低维方向信号（意图），来偏置学习型航点预测策略，解决局部感知驱动控制与全局拓扑引导之间的差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于物体级拓扑地图的导航方法存在全局拓扑引导与局部感知驱动控制之间的差距。局部决策仅基于当前自我中心观测，缺乏视野外信息，导致机器人可能持续朝向远离目标的方向移动，无法减少全局拓扑距离。

Method: 提出IntentReact框架，将全局拓扑引导编码为低维方向信号（意图），该意图条件化一个学习型航点预测策略，使导航偏向拓扑一致的方向。这种设计使机器人能够在局部观测误导时及时重新定向，同时保持物体中心控制的反应性和鲁棒性。

Result: 通过大量实验评估，相比先前的物体中心导航方法，该框架在导航成功率和执行质量方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: IntentReact通过紧凑的意图接口有效桥接了全局拓扑规划与反应性物体中心控制之间的差距，提高了物体目标视觉导航的性能，特别是在局部观测可能误导导航方向的情况下。

Abstract: Object-goal visual navigation requires robots to reason over semantic structure and act effectively under partial observability. Recent approaches based on object-level topological maps enable long-horizon navigation without dense geometric reconstruction, but their execution remains limited by the gap between global topological guidance and local perception-driven control. In particular, local decisions are made solely from the current egocentric observation, without access to information beyond the robot's field of view. As a result, the robot may persist along its current heading even when initially oriented away from the goal, moving toward directions that do not decrease the global topological distance. In this work, we propose IntentReact, an intent-conditioned object-centric navigation framework that introduces a compact interface between global topological planning and reactive object-centric control. Our approach encodes global topological guidance as a low-dimensional directional signal, termed intent, which conditions a learned waypoint prediction policy to bias navigation toward topologically consistent progression. This design enables the robot to promptly reorient when local observations are misleading, guiding motion toward directions that decrease global topological distance while preserving the reactivity and robustness of object-centric control. We evaluate the proposed framework through extensive experiments, demonstrating improved navigation success and execution quality compared to prior object-centric navigation methods.

</details>


### [175] [UMBRELLA: Uncertainty-aware Multi-robot Reactive Coordination under Dynamic Temporal Logic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25395)
*Qisheng Zhao,Meng Guo,Hengxuan Du,Lars Lindemann,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMBRELLA框架通过结合蒙特卡洛树搜索和不确定性感知的rollouts，使用Conformal Prediction建模目标运动不确定性，在满足时空约束条件下最小化多机器人系统协作任务的平均完工时间风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人系统方法通常假设静态任务特征或在环境变化时简单重新规划，无法有效处理动态移动目标的协作任务。需要开发能够处理目标运动不确定性并满足时空约束的协调框架。

Method: 提出UMBRELLA框架：1) 使用Conformal Prediction建模目标运动预测的不确定性；2) 结合蒙特卡洛树搜索与不确定性感知的rollouts；3) 引入CP-based度量指导搜索加速；4) 采用滚动时域规划方案动态调整任务分配；5) 确保任务间的时空约束，仅需部分同步。

Result: 大规模仿真和硬件实验表明，相比静态基线方法，UMBRELLA框架将平均完工时间减少23%，方差减少71%，显著提升了多机器人系统处理动态目标协作任务的性能。

Conclusion: UMBRELLA框架通过有效建模目标运动不确定性、结合MCTS与CP-based度量、采用滚动时域规划，成功解决了多机器人系统在动态移动目标协作任务中的协调问题，显著降低了完工时间及其风险。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can be extremely efficient for accomplishing team-wise tasks by acting concurrently and collaboratively. However, most existing methods either assume static task features or simply replan when environmental changes occur. This paper addresses the challenging problem of coordinating multi-robot systems for collaborative tasks involving dynamic and moving targets. We explicitly model the uncertainty in target motion prediction via Conformal Prediction(CP), while respecting the spatial-temporal constraints specified by Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). The proposed framework (UMBRELLA) combines the Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over partial plans with uncertainty-aware rollouts, and introduces a CP-based metric to guide and accelerate the search. The objective is to minimize the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) of the average makespan. For tasks released online, a receding-horizon planning scheme dynamically adjusts the assignments based on updated task specifications and motion predictions. Spatial and temporal constraints among the tasks are always ensured, and only partial synchronization is required for the collaborative tasks during online execution. Extensive large-scale simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate substantial reductions in both the average makespan and its variance by 23% and 71%, compared with static baselines.

</details>


### [176] [System Design for Maintaining Internal State Consistency in Long-Horizon Robotic Tabletop Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25405)
*Guangyu Zhao,Ceyao Zhang,Chengdong Ma,Tao Wu,Yiyang Song,Haoxuan Ru,Yifan Zhong,Ruilin Yan,Lingfeng Li,Ruochong Li,Yu Li,Xuyuan Han,Yun Ding,Ruizhang Jiang,Xiaochuan Zhang,Yichao Li,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang,Yitao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究了长时程桌面游戏中机器人系统的状态一致性维护问题，提出了一种集成架构，通过显式状态管理、模块分区和恢复机制来防止错误传播，并在麻将游戏中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程桌面游戏对机器人系统提出了独特的挑战：微小的感知或执行错误会破坏累积的任务状态，在决策模块间传播，最终导致交互失败。需要系统级设计而非孤立组件改进来维持内部状态一致性。

Method: 提出集成架构：显式维护感知、执行和交互状态；将高层语义推理与时间敏感的感知控制分离；采用经过验证的动作原语和触觉触发恢复机制防止状态损坏；引入交互级监控机制检测回合违规和隐藏信息泄露。

Result: 实现了完整游戏操作，实证分析了故障模式、恢复效果、跨模块错误传播以及硬件算法权衡。结果显示显式分区、监控状态转换和恢复机制对维持长时间可执行一致性至关重要，而单体或未验证的流水线会导致端到端可靠性显著下降。

Conclusion: 系统级设计原则（显式分区、状态转换监控和恢复机制）对长时程回合制交互至关重要。所提出的系统为研究此类交互中的系统级设计原则提供了实证平台。

Abstract: Long-horizon tabletop games pose a distinct systems challenge for robotics: small perceptual or execution errors can invalidate accumulated task state, propagate across decision-making modules, and ultimately derail interaction. This paper studies how to maintain internal state consistency in turn-based, multi-human robotic tabletop games through deliberate system design rather than isolated component improvement. Using Mahjong as a representative long-horizon setting, we present an integrated architecture that explicitly maintains perceptual, execution, and interaction state, partitions high-level semantic reasoning from time-critical perception and control, and incorporates verified action primitives with tactile-triggered recovery to prevent premature state corruption. We further introduce interaction-level monitoring mechanisms to detect turn violations and hidden-information breaches that threaten execution assumptions. Beyond demonstrating complete-game operation, we provide an empirical characterization of failure modes, recovery effectiveness, cross-module error propagation, and hardware-algorithm trade-offs observed during deployment. Our results show that explicit partitioning, monitored state transitions, and recovery mechanisms are critical for sustaining executable consistency over extended play, whereas monolithic or unverified pipelines lead to measurable degradation in end-to-end reliability. The proposed system serves as an empirical platform for studying system-level design principles in long-horizon, turn-based interaction.

</details>


### [177] [Visualizing Impedance Control in Augmented Reality for Teleoperation: Design and User Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25418)
*Gijs van den Brandt,Femke van Beek,Elena Torta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AR可视化阻抗控制器目标位姿及其与机器人末端位移，为无触觉反馈的遥操作提供力觉感知，提升接触丰富任务的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本、仅有运动控制的遥操作接口缺乏触觉反馈，操作者无法直接感知或调节接触力，限制了接触丰富操作任务的性能

Method: 提出AR可视化阻抗控制器目标位姿及其与机器人末端位移的方法，通过视觉方式传达控制器产生的力，为操作者提供直观的实时反馈

Result: 在17名参与者的双臂操作研究中，AR可视化使力关键提升任务的完成时间减少24%，但对滑动任务无显著影响

Conclusion: 通过AR使阻抗目标可见是改善接触丰富遥操作中人机交互的可行方法，无需昂贵的触觉硬件

Abstract: Teleoperation for contact-rich manipulation remains challenging, especially when using low-cost, motion-only interfaces that provide no haptic feedback. Virtual reality controllers enable intuitive motion control but do not allow operators to directly perceive or regulate contact forces, limiting task performance. To address this, we propose an augmented reality (AR) visualization of the impedance controller's target pose and its displacement from each robot end effector. This visualization conveys the forces generated by the controller, providing operators with intuitive, real-time feedback without expensive haptic hardware. We evaluate the design in a dual-arm manipulation study with 17 participants who repeatedly reposition a box with and without the AR visualization. Results show that AR visualization reduces completion time by 24% for force-critical lifting tasks, with no significant effect on sliding tasks where precise force control is less critical. These findings indicate that making the impedance target visible through AR is a viable approach to improve human-robot interaction for contact-rich teleoperation.

</details>


### [178] [Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25462)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,John Zhang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TDDM提出了一种时间解耦的扩散模型，通过噪声作为掩码的范式将轨迹分割为不同噪声水平的段，以处理动态城市环境中近远期规划的异质时间依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型将轨迹视为整体实体，忽略了异质时间依赖性：近期规划受瞬时动态约束，远期规划受导航目标约束。需要平衡即时安全与长期目标。

Method: 提出时间解耦扩散模型(TDDM)：1) 噪声作为掩码范式，将轨迹分割为具有独立噪声水平的段；2) 时间解耦自适应层归一化(TD-AdaLN)注入段特定时间步；3) 推理时使用非对称时间分类器自由引导，利用弱噪声远期先验指导近期路径生成。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，TDDM接近或超过最先进基线，在具有挑战性的Test14-hard子集上表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: TDDM通过时间解耦的扩散建模有效处理了动态城市环境中轨迹规划的异质时间依赖性，在保持长期目标的同时优化了即时安全决策。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic urban environments requires balancing immediate safety with long-term goals. While diffusion models effectively capture multi-modal decision-making, existing approaches treat trajectories as monolithic entities, overlooking heterogeneous temporal dependencies where near-term plans are constrained by instantaneous dynamics and far-term plans by navigational goals. To address this, we propose Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Model (TDDM), which reformulates trajectory generation via a noise-as-mask paradigm. By partitioning trajectories into segments with independent noise levels, we implicitly treat high noise as information voids and weak noise as contextual cues. This compels the model to reconstruct corrupted near-term states by leveraging internal correlations with better-preserved temporal contexts. Architecturally, we introduce a Temporally Decoupled Adaptive Layer Normalization (TD-AdaLN) to inject segment-specific timesteps. During inference, our Asymmetric Temporal Classifier-Free Guidance utilizes weakly noised far-term priors to guide immediate path generation. Evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark show TDDM approaches or exceeds state-of-the-art baselines, particularly excelling in the challenging Test14-hard subset.

</details>


### [179] [Towards Generalizable Robotic Data Flywheel: High-Dimensional Factorization and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25583)
*Yuyang Xiao,Yifei Zhou,Haoran Wang,Wenxuan Ou,Yuxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: F-ACIL提出了一种启发式因子感知组合迭代学习框架，通过结构化数据分解和组合泛化来解决机器人学习中的数据多样性和效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人模型面临数据多样性不足和数据效率有限的问题，而系统化的数据收集和整理策略尚未充分探索。任务多样性源于多个维度上稀疏分布的隐含因素，这些因素难以明确定义。

Method: F-ACIL将数据分布分解为结构化因子空间（如物体、动作、环境），基于此开发了因子级数据收集和迭代训练范式，促进在高维因子空间上的组合泛化。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，F-ACIL相比无策略方法，使用5-10倍更少的演示数据就能实现超过45%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化因子分解为真实世界机器人学习中的高效组合泛化提供了实用途径，F-ACIL能够启发更多关于构建可泛化机器人数据飞轮策略的系统性研究。

Abstract: The lack of sufficiently diverse data, coupled with limited data efficiency, remains a major bottleneck for generalist robotic models, yet systematic strategies for collecting and curating such data are not fully explored. Task diversity arises from implicit factors that are sparsely distributed across multiple dimensions and are difficult to define explicitly. To address this challenge, we propose F-ACIL, a heuristic factor-aware compositional iterative learning framework that enables structured data factorization and promotes compositional generalization. F-ACIL decomposes the data distribution into structured factor spaces such as object, action, and environment. Based on the factorized formulation, we develop a factor-wise data collection and an iterative training paradigm that promotes compositional generalization over the high-dimensional factor space, leading to more effective utilization of real-world robotic demonstrations. With extensive real-world experiments, we show that F-ACIL can achieve more than 45% performance gains with 5-10$\times$ fewer demonstrations comparing to that of which without the strategy. The results suggest that structured factorization offers a practical pathway toward efficient compositional generalization in real-world robotic learning. We believe F-ACIL can inspire more systematic research on building generalizable robotic data flywheel strategies. More demonstrations can be found at: https://f-acil.github.io/

</details>


### [180] [Accurate Surface and Reflectance Modelling from 3D Radar Data with Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25623)
*Judith Treffler,Vladimír Kubelka,Henrik Andreasson,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经隐式表示的雷达点云3D重建方法，联合建模场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度，在稀疏噪声雷达数据上实现更平滑准确的表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在低能见度环境（雾、烟、灰尘）中比相机和激光雷达更具优势，但雷达数据固有的稀疏性和噪声特性使得可靠的3D表面重建具有挑战性。现有方法在处理雷达数据时效果有限，需要专门针对雷达特性的重建方法。

Method: 采用神经隐式方法进行雷达点云的3D映射，联合建模场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度。使用内存高效的混合特征编码学习连续的符号距离场（SDF）进行表面重建，同时捕获雷达特定的反射特性。

Result: 相比将现有激光雷达重建方法应用于雷达数据，该方法能产生更平滑、更准确的3D表面重建，并能重建视角相关的雷达强度。实验表明，随着输入点云变得更稀疏，神经隐式表示相比传统显式SDF和网格化技术能渲染出更真实的表面。

Conclusion: 神经隐式表示方法能有效处理雷达数据的稀疏性和噪声问题，在低能见度环境下为自主系统提供鲁棒的场景表示，在稀疏点云条件下相比传统方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: Robust scene representation is essential for autonomous systems to safely operate in challenging low-visibility environments. Radar has a clear advantage over cameras and lidars in these conditions due to its resilience to environmental factors such as fog, smoke, or dust. However, radar data is inherently sparse and noisy, making reliable 3D surface reconstruction challenging. To address these challenges, we propose a neural implicit approach for 3D mapping from radar point clouds, which jointly models scene geometry and view-dependent radar intensities. Our method leverages a memory-efficient hybrid feature encoding to learn a continuous Signed Distance Field (SDF) for surface reconstruction, while also capturing radar-specific reflective properties. We show that our approach produces smoother, more accurate 3D surface reconstructions compared to existing lidar-based reconstruction methods applied to radar data, and can reconstruct view-dependent radar intensities. We also show that in general, as input point clouds get sparser, neural implicit representations render more faithful surfaces, compared to traditional explicit SDFs and meshing techniques.

</details>


### [181] [A Mentalistic Interface for Probing Folk-Psychological Attribution to Non-Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25646)
*Giulio Pisaneschi,Pierpaolo Serio,Estelle Gerbier,Andrea Dan Ryals,Lorenzo Pollini,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个实验平台，用于研究人类对非人形机器人意向状态的归因，通过保持行为不变但改变解释框架（心理主义、目的论、机制论）来探究语言和框架如何影响对机器人采用意向立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类如何将意向状态归因于非人形机器人，理解语言和解释框架在塑造对机器人采用意向立场中的作用，为机器人交互设计提供理论基础。

Method: 开发了一个结合模拟机器人、真实任务环境和基于大语言模型的解释层的实验平台。平台能够用心理主义（mentalistic）、目的论（teleological）或机制论（mechanistic）三种不同术语描述相同行为，通过保持行为恒定但改变解释框架来研究意向立场采纳。

Result: 创建了一个可控的实验平台，能够系统研究解释框架如何影响人类对非人形机器人意向状态的归因，为意向立场研究提供了标准化工具。

Conclusion: 该平台为研究语言和框架在塑造人类对机器人意向状态归因中的作用提供了重要工具，有助于理解人类如何在不同解释框架下对非人形机器人采用意向立场，对机器人交互设计具有理论和实践意义。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental platform for studying intentional-state attribution toward a non-humanoid robot. The system combines a simulated robot, realistic task environments, and large language model-based explanatory layers that can express the same behavior in mentalistic, teleological, or mechanistic terms. By holding behavior constant while varying the explanatory frame, the platform provides a controlled way to investigate how language and framing shape the adoption of the intentional stance in robotics.

</details>


### [182] [Fast-dVLA: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion VLA to Real-Time Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25661)
*Wenxuan Song,Jiayi Chen,Shuai Chen,Jingbo Wang,Pengxiang Ding,Han Zhao,Yikai Qin,Xinhu Zheng,Donglin Wang,Yan Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦辅助任务训练目标的方法，通过在小规模任务集上使用两种不同训练策略获得能力向量，与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型，结合轻量正交正则化损失，在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在标准监督微调（SFT）中往往难以有效提升性能并降低适应成本。现有辅助训练方法虽然能提升性能并减少收敛步数，但会因额外的辅助任务损失带来显著计算开销。需要同时实现辅助训练的能力增强效果和标准SFT的简洁性。

Method: 1. 在参数空间中解耦辅助任务训练的两个目标：增强通用能力和拟合任务特定动作分布。2. 在小规模任务集上使用两种不同训练策略使模型收敛，得到的能力差异参数解释为辅助任务提供的能力向量。3. 将这些向量与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型。4. 标准SFT中增加轻量正交正则化损失，使合并模型在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在各种机器人任务中非常有效，能够在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了预训练VLA模型在标准SFT中性能提升有限和适应成本高的问题，通过解耦辅助任务训练目标并提取能力向量，实现了计算效率与性能的平衡，为机器人任务的高效适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach to address the challenge that pretrained VLA models often fail to effectively improve performance and reduce adaptation costs during standard supervised finetuning (SFT). Some advanced finetuning methods with auxiliary training objectives can improve performance and reduce the number of convergence steps. However, they typically incur significant computational overhead due to the additional losses from auxiliary tasks. To simultaneously achieve the enhanced capabilities of auxiliary training with the simplicity of standard SFT, we decouple the two objectives of auxiliary task training within the parameter space, namely, enhancing general capabilities and fitting task-specific action distributions. To deliver this goal, we only need to train the model to converge on a small-scale task set using two distinct training strategies. The difference between the resulting model parameters can then be interpreted as capability vectors provided by auxiliary tasks. These vectors are then merged with pretrained parameters to form a capability-enhanced meta model. Moreover, when standard SFT is augmented with a lightweight orthogonal regularization loss, the merged model attains performance comparable to auxiliary finetuned baselines with reduced computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach is highly effective across diverse robot tasks. Project page: https://chris1220313648.github.io/Fast-dVLA/

</details>


### [183] [Can Users Specify Driving Speed? Bench2Drive-Speed: Benchmark and Baselines for Desired-Speed Conditioned Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25672)
*Yuqian Shao,Xiaosong Jia,Langechuan Liu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bench2Drive-Speed：一个用于速度条件自动驾驶的基准测试，包含数据集、指标和基线模型，支持用户自定义速度要求和超车指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶系统缺乏用户自定义功能，如指定期望速度或超车/跟车偏好。需要建立系统化的评估框架来研究如何让驾驶策略遵循用户的速度要求。

Method: 提出Bench2Drive-Speed基准，包含：1）速度遵循分数和超车分数等量化指标；2）CustomizedSpeedDataset数据集（2100个专家标注片段）；3）两种监督策略：专家演示数据收集和利用常规驾驶数据重新标注。

Result: 实验表明：1）经过适当重新标注，使用常规驾驶数据训练的模型性能与专家演示数据相当；2）目标速度跟随功能不影响常规驾驶性能；3）超车指令执行仍具挑战性，因交互行为本身复杂。

Conclusion: 速度监督可以通过重新标注现有数据实现，无需额外复杂数据收集。Bench2Drive-Speed为速度条件自动驾驶提供了系统化评估框架，但超车指令执行仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) has achieved remarkable progress. However, one practical and useful function has been long overlooked: users may wish to customize the desired speed of the policy or specify whether to allow the autonomous vehicle to overtake. To bridge this gap, we present Bench2Drive-Speed, a benchmark with metrics, dataset, and baselines for desired-speed conditioned autonomous driving. We introduce explicit inputs of users' desired target-speed and overtake/follow instructions to driving policy models. We design quantitative metrics, including Speed-Adherence Score and Overtake Score, to measure how faithfully policies follow user specifications, while remaining compatible with standard autonomous driving metrics. To enable training of speed-conditioned policies, one approach is to collect expert demonstrations that strictly follow speed requirements, an expensive and unscalable process in the real world. An alternative is to adapt existing regular driving data by treating the speed observed in future frames as the target speed for training. To investigate this, we construct CustomizedSpeedDataset, composed of 2,100 clips annotated with experts demonstrations, enabling systematic investigation of supervision strategies. Our experiments show that, under proper re-annotation, models trained on regular driving data perform comparably to on expert demonstrations, suggesting that speed supervision can be introduced without additional complex real-world data collection. Furthermore, we find that while target-speed following can be achieved without degrading regular driving performance, executing overtaking commands remains challenging due to the inherent difficulty of interactive behaviors. All code, datasets and baselines are available at https://github.com/Thinklab-SJTU/Bench2Drive-Speed

</details>


### [184] [Persistent Robot World Models: Stabilizing Multi-Step Rollouts via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25685)
*Jai Bardhan,Patrik Drozdik,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种强化学习后训练方案，通过自回归滚动训练世界模型，结合多视角视觉保真度奖励，显著提升机器人世界模型的长期预测质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动作条件的机器人世界模型在自回归部署时存在误差累积问题，导致视觉质量迅速退化，需要改进长期预测能力

Method: 1) 引入RL后训练方案，在自回归滚动而非真实历史上训练世界模型；2) 设计训练协议生成并比较同一状态下的多个候选变长未来；3) 开发高效的多视角视觉保真度奖励，结合互补感知指标；4) 采用对比性RL目标并证明其收敛保证

Result: 在DROID数据集上建立新的最先进水平：LPIPS降低14%（外部相机），SSIM提升9.1%（腕部相机），赢得98%的配对比较，在盲人研究中获得80%偏好率

Conclusion: 通过强化学习后训练和多视角保真度奖励，显著提升了机器人世界模型的长期自回归预测质量，解决了误差累积问题

Abstract: Action-conditioned robot world models generate future video frames of the manipulated scene given a robot action sequence, offering a promising alternative for simulating tasks that are difficult to model with traditional physics engines. However, these models are optimized for short-term prediction and break down when deployed autoregressively: each predicted clip feeds back as context for the next, causing errors to compound and visual quality to rapidly degrade. We address this through the following contributions. First, we introduce a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training scheme that trains the world model on its own autoregressive rollouts rather than on ground-truth histories. We achieve this by adapting a recent contrastive RL objective for diffusion models to our setting and show that its convergence guarantees carry over exactly. Second, we design a training protocol that generates and compares multiple candidate variable-length futures from the same rollout state, reinforcing higher-fidelity predictions over lower-fidelity ones. Third, we develop efficient, multi-view visual fidelity rewards that combine complementary perceptual metrics across camera views and are aggregated at the clip level for dense, low-variance training signal. Fourth, we show that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art for rollout fidelity on the DROID dataset, outperforming the strongest baseline on all metrics (e.g., LPIPS reduced by 14% on external cameras, SSIM improved by 9.1% on the wrist camera), winning 98% of paired comparisons, and achieving an 80% preference rate in a blind human study.

</details>


### [185] [Intelligent Navigation and Obstacle-Aware Fabrication for Mobile Additive Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25688)
*Yifei Li,Ruizhe Fu,Huihang Liu,Guha Manogharan,Feng Ju,Ilya Kovalenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将导航与材料沉积耦合的通用移动打印-配送平台，通过实时控制框架解决动态制造环境中移动增材制造机器人的导航稳定性和打印质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模定制需求增长，制造系统需要更灵活适应个性化产品生产。增材制造虽能按需制造定制组件，但其灵活性受固定设备布局限制。移动机器人集成可解决此问题，但动态制造环境中的障碍物和地形不平整等干扰会影响导航稳定性和打印质量。

Method: 开发一个实时控制框架，将导航与材料沉积过程耦合（而非分开处理），通过闭环集成传感、移动性和制造，为运动和过程控制提供实时反馈。框架包括机器人导航规划与控制，确保安全运动、避障和路径稳定性，同时保持打印质量。

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界实验验证了框架的适应性，测试了其对轨迹变化和外部干扰的响应能力。耦合导航与打印使移动增材制造机器人能够规划安全、自适应轨迹，提高了制造灵活性和适应性。

Conclusion: 提出的通用移动打印-配送平台成功解决了动态制造环境中移动增材制造机器人的挑战，通过将导航与材料沉积过程耦合，实现了自主决策能力，为大规模定制生产提供了更灵活、自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: As the demand for mass customization increases, manufacturing systems must become more flexible and adaptable to produce personalized products efficiently. Additive manufacturing (AM) enhances production adaptability by enabling on-demand fabrication of customized components directly from digital models, but its flexibility remains constrained by fixed equipment layouts. Integrating mobile robots addresses this limitation by allowing manufacturing resources to move and adapt to changing production requirements. Mobile AM Robots (MAMbots) combine AM with mobile robotics to produce and transport components within dynamic manufacturing environments. However, the dynamic manufacturing environments introduce challenges for MAMbots. Disturbances such as obstacles and uneven terrain can disrupt navigation stability, which in turn affects printing accuracy and surface quality. This work proposes a universal mobile printing-and-delivery platform that couples navigation and material deposition, addressing the limitations of earlier frameworks that treated these processes separately. A real-time control framework is developed to plan and control the robot's navigation, ensuring safe motion, obstacle avoidance, and path stability while maintaining print quality. The closed-loop integration of sensing, mobility, and manufacturing provides real-time feedback for motion and process control, enabling MAMbots to make autonomous decisions in dynamic environments. The framework is validated through simulations and real-world experiments that test its adaptability to trajectory variations and external disturbances. Coupled navigation and printing together enable MAMbots to plan safe, adaptive trajectories, improving flexibility and adaptability in manufacturing.

</details>


### [186] [SoftMimicGen: A Data Generation System for Scalable Robot Learning in Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25725)
*Masoud Moghani,Mahdi Azizian,Animesh Garg,Yuke Zhu,Sean Huver,Ajay Mandlekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftMimicGen：用于可变形物体操作的自动化数据生成管道，通过高保真仿真环境生成合成数据集，减少对真实世界数据的需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人数据集难以收集和扩展，需要大量人力、时间和成本。虽然仿真和合成数据生成已成为有效替代方案，但现有方法主要局限于刚体任务，而可变形物体操作在真实世界操作中占很大比例，是合成仿真数据范式需要解决的关键缺口。

Method: 提出SoftMimicGen自动化数据生成管道，包含一套高保真仿真环境，涵盖多种可变形物体（毛绒玩具、绳索、纸巾、毛巾）和操作行为（高精度穿线、动态挥动、折叠、抓取放置），支持四种机器人构型：单臂机械臂、双臂、人形机器人和手术机器人。
